Method: 提出三种方法：(1) 对比激活引导（在作者层面，用同一内容的风格中性生成作为对比，固定主题）；(2) 预测引导向量的网络；(3) 预测LoRA适配器的超网络。

Result: 风格模仿与输出质量之间存在一致的权衡：微调获得最多风格信号但牺牲流畅度，超网络在已见和未见作者上权衡最佳；引导方法优于基于风格清单的引导；手动提取与预测的引导向量近乎正交但得分相当。

Conclusion: 作者层面的对比引导无需预定义风格清单即可有效；风格条件化可容纳至少两个不相关的方向，而非要求某一特定轴。

Abstract: Adapting large language models to an individual author's style from a few examples is challenging, and scientific writing sharpens the difficulty: formal conventions leave little surface variation, and authors write about their own topics, so extracted ``style'' easily entangles with content. We study style-conditioned abstract generation from a few example abstracts per author and propose three methods: (1) contrastive activation steering, (2) a network that predicts steering vectors, and (3) a hypernetwork that predicts LoRA adapters. We find a consistent trade-off between style imitation and output quality: fine-tuning buys most of the available style signal but forfeits fluency, while the hypernetwork achieves the best trade-off on both seen and unseen authors. Our steering operates at author level, contrasting an author's abstracts against style-neutral generations for the same content. This holds topic fixed, removes the need for a predefined style inventory, and outperforms inventory-based steering. % [EDIT 1a] softened "no single optimal axis" claim Moreover, our analyses demonstrate that manually extracted and predicted steering vectors are near-orthogonal yet score comparably, indicating that style conditioning here can admit at least two unrelated directions rather than requiring one particular axis.

</details>


### [147] [Hindsight-Guided Rationale Distillation for Rare Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03176)
*Aarav Singh,Animesh Pathak,Navyansh Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究ZebraMap罕见病诊断中hindsight蒸馏:8B教师观察真实诊断生成CoT,微调1.5B学生。过滤"GT泄露"短语后,学生F显著优于教师(p<0.001),但增益小且限于数据充足疾病。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索hindsight-guided蒸馏(教师可见真实答案)在小规模模型上进行罕见病诊断的效果,并厘清其中增益来源。

Method: 用8B教师(生成时可见ground-truth诊断)产生的思维链微调1.5B学生;对比过滤版(StudentF)与未过滤版学生,使用正则过滤GT短语。

Result: 未过滤学生在33.9%案例中复现"ground truth is X"短语,错误标签时准确率严重下降;正则可近乎消除污染。StudentF显著优于教师(p<0.001),未过滤学生不显著(p=0.129);增益集中在代表性好的疾病,整体绝对准确率仍低。

Conclusion: 增益由污染过滤(而非hindsight蒸馏本身)驱动;发现频率依赖的知识迁移及SFT未弥合的校准差距,作为未来方向。

Abstract: We study hindsight-guided distillation for rare disease diagnosis on ZebraMap: a 1.5B student is fine-tuned on chain-of-thought traces from a 8B teacher that observes the ground-truth diagnosis during generation. Absolute accuracy remains low for all models - the task is hard at this scale - but within this ceiling a filtered variant (StudentF) achieves a small, statistically significant accuracy advantage over the teacher (p < 0.001), concentrated in better-represented diseases. The unfiltered student does not significantly outperform the teacher (p = 0.129), establishing that contamination filtering - not hindsight distillation alone - drives the gain. The gap traces to an artifact we term GT hallucination. Label-visible generation causes the teacher to embed "ground truth is X" phrases in its reasoning chain; SFT copies the pattern. At inference, the unfiltered student reproduces the phrase in 33.9% of cases, with severe accuracy degradation when the hallucinated label is wrong. A regex filter removing these slots reduces contamination to near-zero, producing the observed gain - though the effect remains small. We precisely quantify this gain-cost tradeoff, document frequency-dependent knowledge transfer absent from the RL-trained teacher, and characterize a calibration gap that SFT does not close - identifying both as directions for future work.

</details>


### [148] [Not Until the Evidence Says So: Teaching LLM Investigators When to Close a Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03190)
*Tingzhu Bi,Ping Wang,Meng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM调查员在事故/缺陷调查中"证据是否足够结案"的决策，构建Nautil数据集并提出三项评估测试，通过微调与强化学习显著改善模型的结案判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事故、缺陷与故障调查结束时面临一个普通问答不会遇到的决策：现有证据是否足以结案。这一问题缺乏评估手段，且案件来源高度预测标签（仅读来源的规则即达83.0平衡准确率），需要新方法衡量模型能否基于证据而非捷径做判断。

Method: 构建Nautil：731个经审核的案件，来自航空、铁路、海事、化工安全、车辆缺陷报告及生产服务器事故，含教师轨迹、分布外测试集与反事实证据版本。提出三项评估测试：结案准确率（对照来源规则及来源内评估）、证据依赖（移除结论依据后检查模型是否停止结案）、结论与缺口质量（评判模型断言的依据与所指缺失）。并对9B模型进行监督微调，及以结案决策为奖励的强化学习。

Result: 未训练9B模型97%答案夸大证据；前沿模型84%能识别正确原因但91%夸大，并在官方"原因不明"的41案中错误结案17案。微调后移除依据使结案率相对对照下降26个百分点，夸大从97%降至35%，正确且未夸大结论从3%升至43%。强化学习（仅奖励结案决策）将平衡准确率从69.2升至83.3（与教师持平），来源内准确率从60.4升至74.1，但证据依赖性有所下降。

Conclusion: 模型的能力与其结案判断并不绑定：即使能识别正确原因，仍普遍夸大证据并错误结案。基于真实调查轨迹的微调和奖励结案决策的强化学习可显著提升结案判断质量，但准确率提升与证据依赖性之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Accident, defect and outage investigations end with a decision that ordinary question answering never faces: whether the evidence gathered so far is enough to close the case. We study this decision for LLM investigators, which request evidence from a case file, revise their hypotheses, and either close the case with a conclusion grounded in what they read or leave it open and name what is missing. This judgment does not come with capability: an untrained 9B model overstates its evidence in 97% of its answers, and a frontier model that identifies the right cause in 84% of cases still overstates in 91% and closes 17 of the 41 cases whose official finding is "cause undetermined". Measuring it is also non-trivial: the source of a case largely predicts its label, and a rule that reads only the source reaches 83.0 balanced accuracy on our test cases. We therefore evaluate closure with three tests: closure accuracy, reported against this rule and within each source; evidence dependence, which removes the grounds of a conclusion and checks whether the model stops closing; and conclusion and gap quality, a judged checklist of what the model asserts and what it says is missing. We build Nautil, 731 audited cases from aviation, rail, maritime, chemical-safety and vehicle-defect reports and production server incidents, with teacher trajectories, an out-of-distribution test set and counterfactual evidence versions. Fine-tuning a 9B model on these trajectories makes its closures follow the evidence: removing the grounds lowers its closure rate by 26 points relative to a matched control, overstatement falls from 97% to 35%, and correct, non-overstated conclusions rise from 3% to 43%. Reinforcement learning that rewards only the closure decision then raises balanced accuracy from 69.2 to 83.3, on par with the teacher, and within-source accuracy from 60.4 to 74.1, at some cost in evidence dependence.

</details>


### [149] [Source Preference in the Wild: How LLM Agents Favor Items by Source, and How to Reduce It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03195)
*Jonghyun Song,Haewon Park,Jeonghoon Shim,Woojung Song,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了LLM智能体在端到端搜索中的来源偏好：12个模型跨3个领域普遍偏好某些来源，且该偏好可超越商品是否满足需求，并探讨其成因与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体代用户决定买什么、订哪家酒店、引用哪篇论文时，来源偏好会影响用户获得的结果和被选中的来源，但此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在3个领域、12个智能体模型上做端到端搜索实验；控制来源不同但满足相同需求且位置相同的条目进行比较；通过隐藏/重贴来源标签检验来源信息的作用；测试两条路径（训练奖励导致的捷径、信息缺失引发的成见）。

Result: 每个模型在每个领域都偏好某些来源、回避另一些，且模型间大体一致。偏好可压倒需求满足度：偏好来源的较差条目（少满足一项需求）约三分之二情况下被选中，反向则几乎不会。隐藏来源信息会削弱偏好，重贴为偏好来源可提升选择率。补充缺失信息或用提示对抗成见可减少来源偏好。

Conclusion: 来源偏好在LLM智能体搜索中普遍存在且影响显著，源于训练捷径与信息缺失引发的成见，可通过补充信息或提示缓解。

Abstract: As LLM agents decide on users' behalf which product to buy, which hotel to book, or which paper to cite, a preference for items from certain sources (the sites or services they come from) shapes what users receive and which sources are selected. We study source preference in end-to-end search with 12 agent models across three domains. Comparing items from different sources that satisfy the same requirements at the same position, we find that each model prefers some sources and avoids others in every domain, largely agreeing on which. This preference can outweigh how well items satisfy the request: an item satisfying one requirement fewer is selected about two-thirds of the time when it comes from a preferred source and the better one from a dispreferred source, but almost never in the reverse case. The information identifying an item's source affects selection by itself: hiding it weakens the preference, and relabeling an item with a preferred source raises its selection rate. We test two routes to this preference: training that rewards better items can make a source a shortcut for requirement satisfaction, and missing information can trigger preconceptions about the source. Supplying missing information or a prompt countering these preconceptions reduces source preference.

</details>


### [150] [StanceEval 2026: The Second Stance Detection Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03215)
*Rasha Albalawi,Nuha Albadi,Hamzah Luqman,Asma Yamani,Maram Kurdi,Saad Ezzini,Ahmed Ashraf,Maged Al-Shaibani,Nora Alturayeif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StanceEval 2026 第二届阿拉伯语社交媒体立场检测评测任务概览，聚焦跨目标泛化，设两个赛道，最高 F_avg2 分别达 0.8994 与 0.9400。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立场检测需判断作者对给定主题的态度（支持/反对/中立）。本届关注跨目标泛化能力：模型能否迁移到未见目标，尤其是主题相关与完全跨域两类迁移场景。

Method: 设两条评测赛道：Track 1 为主题相关跨目标迁移（训练于 Women Empowerment，测试于 Women Driving）；Track 2 为跨域迁移至全新目标（E-Cars、Trimester System）。参赛系统涵盖微调预训练语言模型、提示与检索增强 LLM、微调 LLM 及混合级联方法。评价指标为 Favor 与 Against 类的宏平均 F1（F_avg2）。

Result: 80 支队伍注册，来自 12 个国家；评测阶段 30 支提交，Track 1 有效排名 21 支、Track 2 为 13 支，20 支提交系统描述论文。最佳成绩 Track 1 为 0.8994、Track 2 为 0.9400，显著超过最强基线（0.7366 与 0.7475）。

Conclusion: 出乎意料的是未见目标上的表现优于相关目标，可能由目标极化、类别不平衡以及方言或讽刺语义差异导致。该评测为阿拉伯语跨目标立场检测提供了基准与洞察。

Abstract: StanceEval 2026 is the second edition of the StanceEval shared task series on stance detection in Arabic social media text. Stance detection aims to identify a writer's stance toward a given topic. Given a tweet and a target, participating systems must determine whether the writer's stance is Favor, Against, or None. This edition focuses on cross-target generalization across two distinct evaluation tracks: Track 1 evaluates thematically related cross-target transfer (testing on Women Driving, related to Women Empowerment from training data), while Track 2 evaluates cross-domain transfer to completely unseen targets (E-Cars and Trimester System). The shared task attracted 80 registered teams from 12 countries. During the evaluation phase, 30 unique teams submitted entries, with 21 teams officially ranked in Track 1 and 13 in Track 2 following validation filtering, and 20 teams submitting system-description papers. Participating teams employed diverse methodologies, including fine-tuned pretrained language models, prompt-based and retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs), fine-tuned LLMs, and hybrid cascades. Top systems achieved impressive $F_{avg2}$ scores of 0.8994 on Track 1 and 0.9400 on Track 2, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (0.7366 and 0.7475, respectively), where $F_{avg2}$ denotes the macro-averaged F1 score over the Favor and Against classes. Counterintuitively, performance on the unseen targets was higher than on the related target, a disparity could be driven by extreme target polarization, class imbalance, and dialectal or sarcastic nuance across topics.

</details>


### [151] [Collective Bias Mitigation via Model Routing and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03240)
*Mingzhe Du,Luu Anh Tuan,Xiaobao Wu,Yichong Huang,Yue Liu,Dong Huang,Huijun Liu,Bin Ji,Jie M. Zhang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Collective Bias Mitigation (CBM)框架，通过在多LLM间学习细粒度行为并促进知识共享来缓解偏见，实验显示其显著优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛部署于公共卫生、金融和治理领域，需兼顾准确性与社会价值对齐，但其会延续或放大训练数据中的偏见。自去偏依赖单一模型的内在知识，难以应对根深蒂固的刻板印象。

Method: 提出CBM框架，学习细粒度模型行为并促进多LLM间的知识共享；首次系统探索如何有效选择和组合不同LLM以生成更公平的响应，包含Debating和Committee等拓扑结构。

Result: CBM显著优于单模型基线，如top-7设置中Committee将年龄偏见分数从0.25降至0.10；Debating和Committee拓扑均实现显著偏见降低，其中Committee在缓解效果与推理成本间取得平衡。

Conclusion: CBM展现了实现更公平LLM的潜力，多模型协作的去偏方式优于依赖单一模型内在知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in public health, finance, and governance, requiring both accuracy and societal value alignment. Despite recent advances, LLMs often perpetuate or amplify bias embedded in their training data, posing challenges to fairness. While self-debiasing encourages an LLM to identify and correct its own biases, relying on a single model's intrinsic knowledge may be insufficient to address deeply ingrained stereotypes. To address this limitation, we introduce Collective Bias Mitigation (CBM), a framework that alleviates bias by learning fine-grained model behavior and fostering knowledge sharing among diverse LLMs. This work is the first to systematically explore the effective selection and organization of distinct LLMs to cultivate fairer LLM responses. Experiments show CBM substantially outperforms standalone baselines (e.g., in the top-7 setting, Committee lowers the age bias score from 0.25 to 0.10). Our Debating and Committee topologies achieve substantial bias reduction, with the latter balancing mitigation effectiveness and inference cost, highlighting the potential of CBM for fairer LLMs.

</details>


### [152] [Shrome at Touché: Soft-Vote Ensembling and Counter-Causal Augmentation for Causality Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03268)
*Roham Zendehdel Nobari,Shayan Sooratgar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对Touché 2026反因果新闻任务，为检测、抽取、极性判断三个子任务各构建一个模型，在CCNC测试集上取得检测F1 0.869、极性macro-F1 0.817、抽取粒度调整F1 0.728（所有提交中最高）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻句子表面含"caused""led to"等因果线索，却可能否认因果（如"It is falsely believed that X caused Y"）。依赖表面线索的系统会误判为因果并给出错误极性，因此需要专门处理反因果声明。

Method: 检测：微调分类器，并用抽取span去除假阳性的跨任务规则。抽取：集成三个RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF标注器，在解码前平均token级分数而非对span投票。极性：因标注反因果样本稀缺，用LLM按Hagen等人的九种反因果表达模式生成训练句，仅保留通过自动结构校验的样本。

Result: CCNC测试集上检测F1 0.869，极性macro-F1 0.817，组织方final causal-only抽取评估中粒度调整F1 0.728，为包括组织方baseline在内的所有提交中最高。开发集仅用于组件选择与消融实验。

Conclusion: 分任务建模结合跨任务规则、分数级集成与LLM数据增强，可有效应对反因果声明中的表面线索误导及标注稀缺问题，取得领先性能。

Abstract: Touché 2026 extends causality extraction to counter-causal claims: news sentences whose surface form appears causal but whose meaning denies the causation, as in "It is falsely believed that X caused Y." A system that relies on surface cues such as "caused" or "led to" will accept such a sentence as causal and give it the wrong polarity. On the Countercausal News Corpus (CCNC), the task has three subtasks: deciding whether a sentence is causal (detection), locating its cause and effect spans (extraction), and labeling its polarity as procausal, counter-causal, or uncausal. We build one model per subtask. Detection is a fine-tuned classifier with a single cross-task rule that uses the extracted spans to remove false positives. For extraction, we ensemble three RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF taggers by averaging their token-level scores before decoding, rather than voting on the spans each tagger produces. For polarity, where labeled counter-causal examples are scarcest, we add training sentences generated by a large language model prompted with nine patterns of counter-causal expression adapted from Hagen et al., keeping only those that pass automatic structural checks. On the held-out CCNC test set, the system reaches F1 0.869 on detection and macro-F1 0.817 on polarity, and in the organizers' final causal-only evaluation of extraction it scores granularity-adjusted F1 0.728, the highest extraction score among all submissions including the organizers' baseline. The development split is used only for component selection and the ablations reported in the paper.

</details>


### [153] [To Jev or Not? Evaluating the Accuracy and Efficiency of Structured Decision Models for Hate-Speech Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03324)
*Demetris Paschalides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出HATEDECIDE，评估六种结构化决策模型配置在四个仇恨言论数据集上的表现，与专用、零样本、商用和监督基线对比，考察定义提供与问题分解对分类的影响及延迟成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线内容规模使仇恨言论审核困难，LLM又让有害内容更易生成和改编；审核需要高效且能适应不同仇恨言论定义且无需任务特定训练的廉价分类器。

Method: 在四个仇恨言论数据集上评测六种决策模型配置，对比专用审核、零样本、商用和监督基线；测试提供数据集定义或将定义分解为多个问题是否提升分类，并测量延迟与成本。

Result: 商用LLM仅在一个数据集上显著优于所有决策模型；提供定义会改变最多28%的预测但未持续提升分类；分解仅在20%的对比中显著提升。在诊断测试集上，最佳托管决策模型与最佳商用LLM仅差1.6个macro-F1，推理成本约低97%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了低成本审核的机会，同时表明显式标准和额外问题并不能可靠提升分类效果。

Abstract: The scale of online content makes hate-speech moderation challenging, while Large Language Models (LLMs) enable harmful material to be produced and adapted more easily. Moderation therefore requires efficient classifiers that can accommodate different definitions of hate speech. Recent structured decision models accept natural-language criteria and select among specified answers, raising the question of whether they can meet these requirements without task-specific training. We present HATEDECIDE, an evaluation of six decision-model configurations on four hate-speech datasets against specialized moderation, zero-shot, commercial, and supervised baselines. We examine whether supplying a dataset's definition, or decomposing it into multiple questions, improves classification, and we measure their latency and cost. We find that commercial LLMs significantly outperform all decision models on only one dataset. Supplying definitions changes up to 28\% of predictions without consistently improving classification, and decomposition significantly improves performance in only 20\% of the comparisons. On a diagnostic set of test cases, the best hosted decision model comes within 1.6 macro-F1 points of the best commercial LLM at approximately 97\% lower inference cost. These results identify opportunities for inexpensive moderation, while showing that explicit criteria and additional questions do not reliably improve classification.

</details>


### [154] [SyntaxBench: A Statistical Diagnostic Framework for Character-Level Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03329)
*Mohsen Larni,Sobhan Ebrahimi Azar,Pouyan Nahed,Kazem Taghva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SyntaxBench，一个针对字符级推理的诊断基准与统计评估框架，包含六个任务，评测8个开源模型并揭示分词方式与推理模式对准确率的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于对细小语法错误敏感的场景，但字符级推理目前多依赖孤立探针和笼统的准确率来评估，缺乏受控输入、配对检验以及对分词和推理模式的系统分析。

Method: 构建SyntaxBench，含五个核心任务（字符计数、字母包含、回文检测、编辑距离、最长字符串选择）及更难的index_to_span子串抽取压力测试。核心任务使用配对的英文与字符长度匹配的随机字符串输入，index_to_span文档共享200-500词长度带但未做字符长度匹配；全部六任务使用零样本、单样本和四样本提示。评估2B至32B的8个开源模型、11种推理模式配置，报告精确匹配与宽松准确率、Cohen's kappa、配对McNemar检验及优势比、bootstrap置信区间、Kendall's tau、类别条件指标、分词分析和多重比较校正检验。

Result: 三项主要发现：一是分词影响准确率，随机字符串比英文更“字符可见”（1.892 vs. 3.169字符/词元），英文单词占用词元越多字符计数准确率越低；二是推理模式并非普遍有益，Gemma4-31B在接近饱和任务上各模式几乎无变化，而Qwen3.6-27B在回文检测上开启thinking反而更差（四样本时0.952非thinking vs. 0.886 thinking）；三是index_to_span基本未被解决，最佳四样本精确匹配准确率仅6.75%。

Conclusion: 字符级评估需要受控输入、配对检验以及对分词和推理模式的分析，而不能仅依赖笼统的聚合准确率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used where small syntactic errors matter, yet character-level reasoning is still evaluated mostly through isolated probes and aggregate accuracy. We introduce SyntaxBench, a diagnostic benchmark and statistical evaluation framework for character-level reasoning. It contains five core tasks, character counting, letter containment, palindrome detection, edit distance, and longest-string selection, plus index_to_span, a harder substring-extraction stress test. The five core tasks use paired English and character-length-matched random-string inputs. index_to_span documents share a 200-500 word band and are not character-length matched. All six tasks use zero-, one-, and four-shot prompts.
  We evaluate eight open-weight models from 2B to 32B parameters across 11 reasoning-mode configurations. The framework reports exact-match and relaxed accuracy, Cohen's kappa, paired McNemar tests with odds ratios, bootstrap confidence intervals, Kendall's tau, class-conditional metrics, tokenization analysis, and multiple-comparison-corrected tests. Three findings stand out. First, tokenization shapes accuracy: random strings are more character-visible than English strings (1.892 vs. 3.169 characters per token), and character-counting accuracy falls as English words occupy more tokens. Second, reasoning mode is not uniformly helpful: Gemma4-31B is nearly unchanged across modes on the near-saturated tasks, while Qwen3.6-27B is worse with thinking on palindrome detection (0.952 non-thinking vs. 0.886 thinking at four-shot). Third, index_to_span remains largely unsolved; the best four-shot exact-match accuracy is 6.75%.
  Character-level evaluation needs controlled inputs, paired tests, and analyses of tokenization and reasoning mode rather than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>


### [155] [CLIMB: Confidence-Guided Complementary Evidence for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03421)
*Hang Gao,Wujiang Xu,Zhixing Zhang,Kai Mei,Jingyi Yang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLIMB，一个免训练的多模态RAG推理框架，通过互补证据池和置信度控制精炼提升知识密集型VQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在知识密集型视觉问答中常需外部文本证据，但现有RAG依赖Top-K检索或重排，易返回冗余段落，且对答案更新是否有充分证据支持缺乏控制。

Method: CLIMB为免训练推理时框架：先用MMR式目标构建紧凑互补证据池，平衡查询相关性与段落冗余；再在固定池内做置信度控制精炼，由R/E/C批评器按相关性、证据特异性和跨模态对齐评分，并用证据锚定置信度估计器仅在置信度提升时接受更新答案，无需修改检索器或MLLM。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek上持续优于检索增强多模态基线；消融显示互补池、批评器评分和迭代置信度控制精炼均有贡献。

Conclusion: CLIMB提供简单停止准则、减少不必要精炼，且无需训练或改动底层模型，是多模态RAG的有效推理时方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, but knowledge-intensive visual question answering often requires external textual evidence beyond the image and the model's parametric knowledge. Existing multimodal RAG systems commonly rely on Top-$K$ retrieval or reranking, which may return redundant passages and provide limited control over whether an answer update is sufficiently supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose \textit{CLIMB}, a training-free inference-time framework for multimodal RAG. CLIMB first constructs a compact complementary evidence pool using an MMR-style objective that balances query relevance and passage-level redundancy. It then performs confidence-controlled refinement within this fixed pool: an R/E/C critic scores passages by relevance, evidence specificity, and cross-modal alignment, while an evidence-grounded confidence estimator accepts an updated answer only when the estimated confidence increases. This design provides a simple stopping criterion and reduces unnecessary refinement without modifying the underlying retriever or MLLM. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that CLIMB consistently improves over retrieval-augmented multimodal baselines. Ablations further indicate that complementary pooling, critic-based scoring, and iterative confidence-controlled refinement each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [156] [Single-Pass Uncertainty Heads for Claim-Level Hallucination Detection in Persian Medical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03482)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi,Pedram Rostami,Hamidreza Baradaran Kashani,Sadra Hakim,Audrina Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM不确定性头(LUH)框架迁移到基于Aya-Expanse-8B的波斯语医学模型上，构建波斯语claim级幻觉数据集并训练轻量检测头，实现单次推理的幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学语言模型的幻觉检测很重要，但重复采样方法开销大，且现有的不确定性头资源无法直接迁移到新的骨干网络和语言上。

Method: 在168题伊朗医学入学考试上考察响应变异性；基于两个骨干(Gaokerena-V、Gaokerena-R)构建配对的claim级波斯语幻觉数据集(各1600条响应)；在冻结骨干的注意力图和token概率上训练轻量级claim级检测头。

Result: Gaokerena-V的五次运行一致性明显低于Aya-Expanse-8B，Gaokerena-R与之相当。检测头在测试集上PR-AUC为0.4820和0.4652(分别为随机基线的2.30和2.66倍)，ROC-AUC为0.7852和0.7810，且推理时无需检索或重复采样。

Conclusion: 该工作为波斯语医学语言模型的单次claim级不确定性估计提供了初步研究；但测试集较小且标签自动生成。

Abstract: Hallucination detection is particularly important for medical language models, but repeated-sampling approaches are expensive and existing uncertainty-head resources do not directly transfer to a new backbone and language. We adapt the LLM Uncertainty Head (LUH) framework to Aya-Expanse-8B-based Persian medical models, using Gaokerena-V and Gaokerena-R as two previously developed backbones. We first examine response variability on a 168-question Iranian medical entrance examination and observe substantially lower five-run consistency for Gaokerena-V than for Aya-Expanse-8B, whereas Gaokerena-R is comparable to Aya-Expanse-8B. We then construct two paired claim-level hallucination datasets directly in Persian, containing 1,600 responses for each backbone, and train lightweight claim-level heads on frozen backbone attention maps and token probabilities. On held-out test splits, the heads obtain PR-AUCs of 0.4820 and 0.4652, corresponding to 2.30 and 2.66 times their respective random baselines, and ROC-AUCs of 0.7852 and 0.7810. The heads require neither retrieval nor repeated sampling at inference time. These results provide an initial study of single-pass claim-level uncertainty estimation for Persian medical language models; the test splits are small and the labels are automatically generated.

</details>


### [157] [Learning from Repaired Reasoning: Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03515)
*Chenglei Shen,Haoyang Yao,Weijie Yu,Song Jin,Xiao Zhang,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RC-OPD通过修复学生自身推理的根因错误并以此指导学生训练，缓解推理错配与蒸馏陷阱，取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy自蒸馏用参考解作为特权指导，但参考解可能只解释正确解而无法说明学生推理为何失败，导致推理错配；同时全轨迹套用同一经验易引发蒸馏陷阱，无谓约束与实质纠错相互竞争。

Method: RC-OPD针对失败尝试定位最早实质错误、构造局部修正，并将修正的中间结果作为有效前缀的锚点；通过诊断—修复—延续的迭代过程在固定预算内检测后续错误。对到达正确答案的修复链，用失败诊断与纠正目标监督错误片段（根因引导蒸馏），并用导向修复中间结果的推理链支撑有效前缀（锚点引导蒸馏）。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型规模上的大量实验与分析表明，RC-OPD缓解了推理错配与蒸馏陷阱，带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 以根因修复替代参考解指导可更精准地纠正学生推理错误，是提升on-policy自蒸馏效果的有效方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses reference solutions as privileged hindsight to supervise student-generated reasoning trajectories. However, reference-based guidance may explain a correct solution without addressing why the student's own reasoning fails. This reasoning mismatch between the guidance provided and the correction needed can encourage the student to borrow correct conclusions while leaving its reasoning errors unresolved. Moreover, applying the same hindsight throughout the trajectory risks a distillation trap, where unnecessary constraints on valid reasoning compete with correction of substantive errors. To address these issues, we propose Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation (RC-OPD), which uses repairs of the student's own reasoning to provide guidance that addresses its specific errors while building on valid progress. For each failed attempt, RC-OPD locates the earliest substantive error, develops a local correction, and uses the corrected intermediate result as an anchor for the valid prefix. An iterative diagnosis--repair--continuation process tests the repairs through student continuation, identifying further errors within a fixed repair budget. For repair chains that reach a correct answer, root--cause--guided distillation uses failure diagnoses and corrective goals to supervise the erroneous segments, while anchor-guided distillation supports the corresponding valid prefixes with reasoning chains leading to the repaired intermediate results. We evaluate RC-OPD across multiple datasets and model scales. Extensive experiments and analyses show that it mitigates reasoning mismatch and the distillation trap, yielding substantial performance gains.

</details>


### [158] [Structured Composition of Verifiable Atomic Insights for Table-to-Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03525)
*Teng Lin,Xinyu Liu,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ComInsight，将表到报告的洞察发现重构为原子证据的组合，通过多关系洞察图与组合算子生成可验证的复合结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表到报告生成方法依赖顺序式反应型数据代理或直接LLM生成，存在探索偏差：早期局部观察会限制后续动作，使模型过早聚焦局部分析，遗漏跨表、跨维度的证据。

Method: 将洞察发现重构为原子证据的组合。首先定义原子洞察为符合预定义分析模式的最小可执行分析单元，并从数据库模式与内容中枚举所有有效原子洞察；随后组织为多关系洞察图，节点表示已验证的数据事实，边编码逻辑、时序或层次关系；最后用一组组合算子系统地将原子节点融合为更高阶的复合结论。

Result: 每个复合输出都附带可执行SQL和细粒度溯源，确保完全可验证。在InsightBench、DDR-Bench和T2R-Bench三个基准上，ComInsight在事实正确性、新颖性和结构完整性上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: ComInsight为表到报告生成提供了一条可靠、高效且可解释的路径。

Abstract: Table-to-report generation refers to the task of automatically generating article-level analyt- ical reports from relational tables and is an essential capability for automated data science and decision support. Its central challenge lies in systematically discovering verifiable com- posite insights across tables, attributes, and analytical perspectives, and organizing them into coherent, complete, and traceable evidence chains. Existing methods primarily rely on sequential, reactive data agents or direct Large Language Model(LLM) generation. They suffer from exploration bias: early local observations constrain subsequent actions, causing models to focus prematurely on local analyzes and miss cross-table or cross-dimensional evidence. We propose ComInsight, which reformulates insight discovery as the composition of atomic evidences. We first define an atomic insight as the smallest executable analytical unit conforming to a predefined analysis pattern and enumerate all valid atomic insights from database schema and content. These atoms are then organized into a multi-relational insight graph, where nodes represent verified data facts and edges encode logical, temporal, or hierarchical relations. Finally, a set of composition operators systematically fuses atomic nodes into higher-order composite conclusions. Every composite output is accompanied by executable SQL and fine-grained provenance, ensuring full verifiability. Across three benchmarks InsightBench, DDR-Bench, and T2R-Bench, ComInsight consistently outperforms strong baselines in factual correctness, novelty, and structural completeness. We believe ComInsight offers a reliable, efficient, and explainable path toward table-to-report generation.

</details>


### [159] [Author Representation Strategies for Zero-Shot Authorship Attribution: A Comparative Study of LLM-Based and Embedding-Based Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03531)
*Nudrat Habib,Tosin Adewumi,Sana Sabah Al-Azzawi,Marcus Liwicki,Elisa Barney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本作者归属(ZS AA)中作者表示的影响，比较了标签提示、代表性写作样本、LLM生成描述和样式嵌入(LISA)四种策略，并提出两阶段嵌入框架，实验表明LISA效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者归属需要捕捉细粒度的文体特征，在零样本(无任务特定监督)设置下尤为困难。本文旨在探究作者表示对零样本作者归属效果的影响。

Method: 评估了标签-only提示基线及三种作者表示策略：(1)代表性写作样本、(2)LLM生成的描述、(3)样式嵌入(LISA)。前三种通过LLM提示进行归属，嵌入方法用余弦相似度。同时研究提示设计的影响，并提出一种两阶段嵌入归属框架，结合候选空间缩减与嵌入维度选择。

Result: 仅标签的ZS AA效果不佳；引入作者特定表示可持续提升性能。所提两阶段LISA框架整体表现最佳；LLM生成样式描述更紧凑但牺牲一定性能。

Conclusion: 作者表示对ZS AA至关重要；当前开源LLM在缺乏更有效表示学习的情况下仍不足以实现稳健的作者归属。

Abstract: Authorship Attribution (AA) requires capturing fine-grained stylistic characteristics, making it particularly challenging in zero-shot (ZS) settings where no task-specific supervision is available. In this work, we investigate the effect of author representations on ZS AA by evaluating a label-only prompting baseline together with three author representation strategies: representative writing samples, LLM-generated descriptions, and style embeddings (LISA). The first three approaches perform attribution using LLM prompting, while the embedding-based approach uses style embeddings with cosine similarity. We investigate the influence of prompt design and propose a two-stage embedding-based attribution framework that combines candidate space reduction with embedding-dimension selection. The results show that label-only ZS AA is ineffective, while incorporating author-specific representations consistently improves attribution performance. Among the evaluated approaches, the proposed two-stage LISA framework achieves the strongest overall performance, whereas LLM-generated style descriptions provide a substantially more compact representation of author style at the cost of some attribution performance. These findings demonstrate the importance of author representation in ZS AA, while indicating that current open-source LLMs remain insufficient for robust attribution without more effective representation learning.

</details>


### [160] [Writerslogic at PAN 2026: Process over Content for Robust Detection under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03565)
*David L. Condrey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Writerslogic 在 PAN at CLEF 2026 三个共享任务（推理轨迹检测、Voight-Kampff 生成式AI检测、多作者写作风格分析）的系统，提出统一框架：特征鲁棒性由训练-测试分布的支持重叠决定，而非训练集效应量，并据此提出领域锚定/可迁移/不变三分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移下特征鲁棒性的成因尚不清晰，作者希望用支持重叠视角解释为何生成器特异特征在域偏移下失效，而词汇指纹等特征能幸存。

Method: 以支持重叠为核心构建特征三分类体系；三个任务分别采用：源检测用 Opus-Sonnet 一致性、安全分类用查询-拒绝分解；Voight-Kampff 用 DeBERTa-v2(ONNX)+多随机种子 LightGBM(44个域可迁移风格特征)+SVM TF-IDF 的堆叠集成与等渗校准；多作者分析融合字符n-gram谱聚类、归一化压缩距离边界检测与 SmolLM-135M 困惑度变点检测。

Result: 推理轨迹检测：源检测第1名(0.85 macro F1)，安全分类第3名(0.66 macro F1)；Voight-Kampff 最佳配置在 PAN 2026 测试集达 0.891，各评估维度均衡(0.853–0.902)；多作者分析因平台混淆未进入官方评测，仅报告设计与先验预测。

Conclusion: 跨三个任务表明：在域偏移下，衡量生成过程属性的特征优于衡量生成内容属性的特征。

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic systems for three PAN at CLEF 2026 shared tasks (Reasoning Trajectory Detection, Voight-Kampff Generative AI Detection, and Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis), unified by a shared analytical framework: feature robustness under distribution shift is governed by support overlap between training and test distributions, not by training-set effect size. This yields a taxonomy (domain-anchored, domain-portable, domain-invariant) that explains why generator-specific features die under domain shift while vocabulary fingerprints (hapax ratio, Yule's K, Heaps' exponent), compression measures, and character n-grams survive. On Reasoning Trajectory Detection, where training was entirely mathematics and 84 percent of test was unseen domains, the framework guided system design to 1st place in source detection (0.85 macro F1 via Opus-Sonnet agreement) and 3rd place in safety classification (0.66 macro F1 via query-refusal decomposition). For Voight-Kampff, we built a calibrated ensemble of DeBERTa-v2 (ONNX), multi-seed LightGBM with 44 domain-portable stylometric features, and SVM on n-gram TF-IDF, combined via learned stacking with isotonic calibration; the best configuration achieved 0.891 on the PAN 2026 test set with balanced sub-metrics (0.853 to 0.902 across all evaluation dimensions). For Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis, we describe a system fusing spectral clustering over character n-gram similarity graphs, normalized compression distance for local boundary detection, and SmolLM-135M perplexity for neural change-point detection; a platform mix-up meant our run never reached the official evaluation, so we report the design and its a priori predictions. Across all three tasks, features measuring generation process properties are designed to outperform features measuring generated content properties under domain shift.

</details>


### [161] [Writerslogic at the CLEF 2026 SimpleText Track: Multi-Candidate LLM Simplification and Stacked Complexity Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03567)
*David L. Condrey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Writerslogic团队参加CLEF 2026 SimpleText共享任务，构建GPT-4o-mini多候选简化流水线和微调DeBERTa-v3-large NLI模型，在Task 1句子级简化取得SARI 47.43（排名第一），Task 2识别任务获0.8081宏F1（识别赛道第一）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Cochrane系统综述英文及多语言生物医学文本，解决文本简化（Task 1）与复杂性/幻觉识别（Task 2）问题。

Method: Task 1：GPT-4o-mini在不同温度下生成五个简化候选，用无参考评分启发式（奖励压缩、源词保留、Cochrane简明语言词汇使用、词汇简洁性）选优；Task 2：将幻觉检测建模为自然语言推理，在35万标注(源句,候选句)对上微调DeBERTa-v3-large。

Result: Task 1.1：Claude Sonnet 4提交获SARI 47.43、BLEU 14.21，句子级系统排名第一（总榜第三）。Task 2.1：微调DeBERTa达0.8081文档级宏F1（最佳集成0.8085），识别赛道第一、团队总排名第二。Task 2.2：0.804多分类准确率，独立团队排名第二。

Conclusion: 多候选生成+启发式选优在句子级简化表现优异，NLI微调框架有效实现幻觉识别，两项任务均取得领先排名，仅次于AIIR Lab。

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic team's participation in the CLEF 2026 SimpleText shared task, addressing Task 1 (text simplification) and Task 2 (complexity spotting). For Task 1, we develop a multi-candidate generation pipeline using GPT-4o-mini that produces five simplification candidates per sentence at varying temperatures, then selects the best candidate using a reference-free scoring heuristic that rewards compression, source word retention, Cochrane Plain Language Summary vocabulary usage, and lexical simplicity. On Task 1.1 (sentence-level simplification), our Claude Sonnet 4 submission achieves SARI 47.43 and BLEU 14.21, the top-ranked sentence-level system (3rd on the combined Task 1 leaderboard, behind two document-level submissions). For Task 2, we fine-tune a DeBERTa-v3-large NLI model on 350K labeled (source, sentence) pairs, framing hallucination detection as natural language inference. The model reads the most relevant source sentence as premise and the candidate as hypothesis, directly learning to distinguish grounded from hallucinated content. On Task 2.1 (binary overgeneration identification), our fine-tuned DeBERTa system achieves 0.8081 document-level macro F1 (0.8085 in our best ensemble), the top-ranked entry within the identification track and 2nd among teams overall, behind AIIR Lab (0.8197). On Task 2.2 (multi-class error classification), our best submission reaches 0.804 multiclass accuracy, ranking 2nd among unique teams behind AIIR Lab (0.827). We evaluate both tasks on English and multilingual biomedical text from Cochrane systematic reviews.

</details>


### [162] [FALCON: A Model and Dataset Agnostic Framework for Synthetic Data Generation for NL2SQL Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03625)
*Darian Lee,Shannon Rumsey,Jack St. Clair,Xinyi Tang,Aditya Bansal,Yuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FALCON 框架用紧凑开源模型低成本生成真实、含歧义、结构复杂的 NL-to-SQL 训练数据，在复杂查询上超越现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NL-to-SQL 合成数据生成方法忽视自然语言的歧义性，生成的查询过于简化，无法让模型应对真实结构化知识访问的复杂性和用户表达的模糊性。

Method: 结合保留字 SQL 种子（reserved-word seeding）与基于 persona 的提示生成结构复杂查询；用对齐式过滤区分真正错误样例与复杂但有效的查询，以保持难度；全程使用紧凑开源模型、模型与数据库无关，无需外部 API。

Result: 人工评估显示各规模模型生成质量稳定；生成数据在 SQL 复杂度和自然语言丰富度上超过现有基准；难度分层分析表明查询越复杂，FALCON 训练的模型相对基线优势越明显；混合少量现有基准数据可恢复简单查询性能，同时保持复杂查询优势。

Conclusion: FALCON 能低成本、本地化生成高复杂度 NL-to-SQL 训练数据，组织无需外部 API 即可使用，为真实场景结构化知识访问提供有效训练数据方案。

Abstract: Relational databases are among the most widely deployed forms of structured knowledge, and natural language access to them requires grounding language onto schema entities and relations while handling the ambiguity inherent in how people phrase requests. Existing synthetic NL-to-SQL data generation methods largely ignore this ambiguity and produce oversimplified queries that fail to prepare models for the complexity of real-world structured knowledge access. We present FALCON, a framework that generates realistic, ambiguity-aware NL-to-SQL data matching the complexity of challenging real-world benchmarks, at low cost using compact open models. Our approach combines reserved-word SQL seeding and persona-based prompting to generate structurally complex queries, while alignment-based filtering preserves difficulty by distinguishing genuinely incorrect examples from complex but valid queries. Human evaluation confirms consistent high quality across model sizes, and our generated data exceeds existing benchmarks in both SQL complexity and natural language richness. Difficulty-stratified analysis shows models trained on FALCON data increasingly outperform baseline-trained models as query complexity increases, validating our pipeline's success in generating challenging training data. When combined with a small proportion of existing benchmark data, mixed training recovers performance on simpler queries while preserving these advantages on complex ones. The model- and database-agnostic design enables organizations to generate high-complexity NL-to-SQL training data locally without external APIs.

</details>


### [163] [Language Models that Play Chess and Explain Their Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03695)
*Adithya Bhaskar,Jeffrey Cheng,Danqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Queen，一个4B参数的棋类语言模型，既能以特级大师水平下棋，又能解释其走法与计划。它结合静默专家编码器与指令微调语言模型，并通过迭代蒸馏（类Bellman更新）提升解释质量，Elo从1782提升至2697，超越所有前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代棋类引擎棋力超人也保持"沉默"不解释；语言模型虽能生成看似合理的解释，但棋力弱使解释价值受限。作者希望兼得两者：既能达到大师级棋力，又能给出有意义的解释，为已有静默专家编码器的领域提供通用配方。

Method: 提出Queen框架：编码器-解码器架构 + 迭代蒸馏算法。用交叉注意力把静默专家棋类编码器与指令微调语言模型整合，通过问答课程学习从编码器表示中提取棋类概念；再以自然语言版的Bellman更新迭代改进解释——模型分析候选择后的局面并归纳为当前局面的解释，再蒸馏回模型，迭代七轮。

Result: 经七轮迭代，Elo从1782提升至2697（+900多分），棋力和解谜准确率全面超越所有前沿模型，参数却少三个数量级；基于语言模型的评测显示解释流畅，连贯性接近GPT-5.6-Sol（high）。

Conclusion: 该架构与训练流程具有通用性，为在拥有静默专家编码器的领域（如游戏、机器人、计算机操作）应用语言模型提供了可复用的配方。

Abstract: Modern chess engines are silent experts: they play at a superhuman level, but do not offer explanations for their play. On the other hand, language models (LMs) can generate plausible-sounding explanations, but their weak playing strength limits the utility of their explanations. We introduce Queen, a 4B-parameter chess-language model that can explain its moves and plans while playing at the level of a typical Grandmaster. Our novel framework enables domain-specific reasoning through complementary components: an encoder-decoder architecture and an iterative distillation algorithm. This architecture integrates a silent expert chess encoder with an instruction-tuned LM through cross-attention, which we train via a question-answering curriculum to extract chess concepts from the encoder's representations. Building on this domain-adapted model, we iteratively improve its explanations with a natural-language analog of the Bellman update: the model analyzes the positions after its top candidate moves and consolidates them into an explanation of the current position, which is then distilled back into the model. Over seven iterations, our model gains over 900 Elo points (1782 to 2697), substantially surpassing all frontier models on both playing strength and puzzle accuracy, despite containing three orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Furthermore, LM-based evaluations show that our explanations are fluent and approach GPT-5.6-Sol (high) in coherence. The generality of our architecture and training procedure suggests a recipe for applying language models to domains where silent expert encoders are available, like games, robotics, and computer use.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [SimuVerity: Benchmarking Agents for Engineering-Grade Simulink Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02304)
*Ruiqi Zhang,Jiahao Wang,Mingxuan Li,Haichen Luo,Chaoting Wang,Guoyu Mou,Keyu Lai,Hanchao Lv,Jiaxu Wang,Yibo Zheng,Aijun Yang,Xiaohua Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SimuVerity基准，含101个跨十领域文本到可执行Simulink模型生成任务，通过分层评估器检验工程合格性与六维性能；最佳系统总分仅42.86，表明结构相似度无法代理工程性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Simulink基准仅评估模型能否编译、运行或是否相似于参考模型，无法判断模型是否满足工程需求。缺乏系统评估智能体工程能力的手段。

Method: 构建SimuVerity：101个文本到可执行Simulink模型生成任务，覆盖十个工程领域。每个任务用可执行系统profile锚定工程规格与四类原生仿真场景。分层评估器先检查交付物、原生可执行性与工程合格性，再对合格模型从六维打分：准确性、输出质量、机理保真度、控制与因果完整性、工况鲁棒性、动态响应。

Result: 评估六个智能体系统，最高总分仅42.86。结构相似度对工程性能是差代理；瓶颈既在生成合格实现，也在合格后满足多维需求。部分高分模型仍存在严重视觉布局混乱。

Conclusion: SimuVerity为评估智能体工程能力、诊断可执行Simulink模型生成失败提供了系统化依据。

Abstract: Existing Simulink benchmarks mainly evaluate whether generated models compile, execute, or resemble a reference model. These criteria do not establish whether a model satisfies its engineering requirements. We introduce SimuVerity, a benchmark of 101 text-to-executable Simulink model-generation tasks across ten engineering domains. For each task, executable-system profiles ground the engineering specification and four families of native simulation scenarios. A hierarchical evaluator first checks artifact delivery, native executability, and engineering qualification, then scores qualified models across six dimensions covering accuracy, output quality, mechanistic fidelity, control and causal integrity, operating-domain robustness, and dynamic response. We evaluate six agent systems with SimuVerity. The best system achieves an overall score of only 42.86. The results show that structural similarity is a poor proxy for engineering performance: capability bottlenecks arise both in producing qualified implementations and in satisfying multidimensional requirements after qualification. Meanwhile, some high-scoring models still exhibit severe visual-layout disorder. SimuVerity provides a systematic basis for assessing agents' engineering capabilities and diagnosing failures in executable Simulink model generation.

</details>


### [165] [Compound AI System Reliability: A Failure Taxonomy and Resilience Pattern Catalog from 150 Production Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02503)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文分析150个生产事故报告，构建了复合AI系统失败模式的分类体系（23种失败模式、5大类别），并提出经故障注入实验验证的韧性模式（如断路器、质量门、组件隔离），为工程实践提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI系统的可靠性挑战源于组件边界处的失败模式（级联错误、隐性质量退化、协调失败），这些无法通过单模型视角理解，而现有系统缺乏针对性分析与监控手段。

Method: 分析150个来自开源复合AI项目和企业部署的生产事故报告，归纳出23种失败模式并划分为5类（检索、生成、工具、编排、集成），针对每类提出韧性模式，并通过受控故障注入实验测量其有效性。

Result: 断路器减少级联传播89%；输出质量门在用户受影响前捕获73%的隐性退化；组件隔离减少爆炸半径64%；采用3种以上韧性模式的系统相比无结构监控基线，MTTR降低71%。

Conclusion: 论文发布事故分类体系与韧性模式目录，作为面向实践者的工程资源，帮助提升复合AI系统的部署可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Deploying compound AI systems reliably and safely requires understanding failure modes that emerge at component boundaries, not within individual models. Cascading errors propagate across component boundaries, silent quality degradation evades standard monitoring, and coordination failures yield incorrect collective behavior from individually correct parts. We analyze 150 production incident reports from open-source compound AI projects and anonymized enterprise deployments to construct a taxonomy of 23 failure modes organized into five categories: retrieval failures, generation failures, tool failures, orchestration failures, and integration failures. For each category, we propose resilience patterns with measured effectiveness from controlled fault injection experiments. Circuit breakers reduce cascade propagation by 89%, output quality gates catch 73% of silent degradation before user impact, and component isolation reduces blast radius by 64%. Systems implementing three or more resilience patterns from our catalog reduce mean-time-to-recovery (MTTR) by 71% compared to unstructured monitoring baselines. We release the incident taxonomy and pattern catalog as a practitioner resource.

</details>


### [166] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Evaluation Metrics for Combinatorial Interaction Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02560)
*Lisha Qin,Chenhui Cui,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Shikai Guo,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标，并通过大规模实证研究分析各指标与故障检测效果的相关性，为CIT从业者选择合适评估指标提供实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估指标是组合交互测试(CIT)的核心组成部分，用于评估和比较组合测试套件及各类测试生成技术。然而指标种类繁多，从业者难以选择合适的指标，且此前没有工作系统性地解决这一问题。

Method: 首先对组合测试套件的黑盒评估指标进行全面综述，提供严格定义、清晰分类、示例说明和复杂度分析；随后在8个开源项目、32个测试场景、295,624个组合测试套件上开展实证研究，使用两种相关性度量考察每个静态评估指标与故障检测效果之间的相关性。

Result: VCC(值组合覆盖)指标可作为测试套件评估的有效预测因子；基于分布的指标通常比基于交互覆盖的指标计算成本更低；合适的指标选择还需考虑测试套件固有属性，这些特性会显著影响评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了CIT评估指标系统性研究的空白，提供了实践指南以帮助CIT从业者选择合适指标来评估或比较组合测试套件。

Abstract: Combinatorial interaction testing (CIT) is a black-box testing method that has received extensive attention in both research and practice over recent years. Its primary objective is to construct an effective combinatorial test suite that detects software failures caused by parameter interactions. As a fundamental component of the CIT testing process, the evaluation metric plays a critical role in assessing and comparing combinatorial test suites, as well as in evaluating various test generation techniques. For CIT practitioners, selecting an appropriate evaluation metric is both important and challenging, given the wide variety of available options. Nevertheless, no prior work has systematically addressed this problem. To fill this gap, this paper first provides a comprehensive survey of black-box evaluation metrics for combinatorial test suites, offering rigorous definitions, clear classifications, illustrative examples, and complexity analyses. We then conduct an extensive empirical study involving eight open-source projects, encompassing 32 test scenarios and 295,624 combinatorial test suites. In this study, we examine the correlation between each static evaluation metric and fault-detection effectiveness using two correlation measures. Experimental results show that the Value Combination Coverage (VCC) metric serves as a valid predictor for test-suite evaluation. However, distribution-based metrics generally incur lower computational costs than interaction coverage-based ones. The choice of an appropriate metric should also account for the test suite's inherent properties, as these characteristics can substantially influence the effectiveness of the evaluation. Finally, we provide practical guidelines to assist CIT practitioners in selecting suitable evaluation metrics for assessing or comparing combinatorial test suites.

</details>


### [167] [Improving the Energy-Efficiency of the Code Generated by LLMs through Effective Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02571)
*Ritika Rekhi,Bing Zhang,Md Arman Islam,Jaya Krishna Pasham,Yeswanth Chitturi,Akshay Paramesha,Isha Valiveti,Asif Imran,Bekir Turkkan,Tevfik Kosar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评估21种提示策略、筛选出8种，在10个LLM上测试Python/C++代码生成的能耗表现，证明提示策略可显著降低生成代码能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程日益主流，但前沿LLM生成的代码很少从能耗角度评估，忽视其环境影响，需要超越功能正确性来评价生成代码的执行效率与能耗。

Method: 全面评估21种面向节能代码生成的提示策略，从中选出8种，在10个主流开源与专有LLM上，针对Python和C++代码生成，与基线提示进行对比实验。

Result: 所选提示策略使Python能耗最多降低25%、C++最多降低17%；模型层面，Python能耗降低最高达50%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）、39%（Claude 4.5 Haiku）、28%（MiniMax M3）；C++降低最高达56%（Granite-4.0-H-Small）、7%（Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct）。效果因模型和语言而异。

Conclusion: 提示策略能显著影响LLM生成代码的能耗，应将能耗纳入LLM代码生成的评估与优化，并为设计更可持续的AI辅助编程提示提供实践指导。

Abstract: As AI-assisted programming becomes increasingly mainstream, the environmental impact of AI-generated software has emerged as an important consideration. This motivates evaluating LLM-generated code beyond functional correctness by considering execution efficiency and energy consumption. However, despite substantial advances in code generation, frontier LLMs are rarely evaluated based on the energy efficiency of the code they produce. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 21 prompting strategies for energy-efficient code generation and identify 8 strategies for evaluation across 10 widely used open-weight and proprietary LLMs. We evaluate their effectiveness for both Python and C++ code generation relative to a baseline prompt. Across the evaluated models, the selected prompting strategies achieved energy reductions of up to 25% for Python and 17% for C++ code generation. At the model level, Python energy reductions reached up to 50% for Granite-4.0-H-Small, 39% for Claude 4.5 Haiku, and 28% for MiniMax M3, while C++ reductions reached up to 56% for Granite-4.0-H-Small and 7% for Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct. These results demonstrate that prompting strategies can substantially influence the energy consumption of LLM-generated code, although their effectiveness varies across models and programming languages. Our findings highlight the importance of incorporating energy efficiency into the evaluation and optimization of LLM-based code generation and provide practical insights into designing prompts for more sustainable AI-assisted programming.

</details>


### [168] [WebUIProof: Benchmarking WebUI Code Generators with UI-Agent Execution Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02617)
*Yun-Yun Tsai,Yuning Mao,Shiqi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Sinong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WebUIProof：面向执行验证的WebUI生成基准，用结构化规范与可执行交互测试，评估8个商业LLM，并可用作RL训练信号提升小模型功能完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估WebUI代码生成困难：现有基准多依赖自由提示与静态检查（构建成功、截图），输出可能编译通过且看似合理，但在用户交互下失败，无法衡量功能正确性。

Method: 提出WebUIProof基准，提供结构化规范与密集可执行交互测试，覆盖通用WebUI与3D交互模拟两类任务；设计UI-agent执行框架，在无头浏览器中以plan-act-observe循环定位DOM元素、执行动作、观察UI/DOM变化并校验断言。

Result: 在8个商业LLM上评估，发现即便页面渲染成功，交互类需求仍频繁失败，3D模拟界面尤为明显；同时验证该框架可提供结果级训练信号。

Conclusion: 以可执行交互测试为核心的评估能揭示静态检查遗漏的功能缺陷；将交互测试转化为RL奖励可训练紧凑模型（Qwen2.5 14B、MIMO 7B），提升功能完成度并减少构建失败。

Abstract: Evaluating WebUI code generation at scale is difficult: outputs may compile and look plausible yet fail under user interaction, and prior benchmarks largely rely on free-form prompts with static checks (build success, screenshots) that miss functional correctness. We introduce WebUIProof, an execution-oriented benchmark that provides structured specifications and dense, executable interaction tests for WebUI generation across two task families: general WebUIs (e.g., dashboards, game, interactive tools) and 3D interactive simulation (e.g., particle/galaxy systems, physics dynamics). WebUIProof includes a UI-agent harness that runs executable interaction tests in a headless browser using an iterative plan--act--observe loop: it locates DOM elements, performs actions, observes resulting UI/DOM changes, and checks the specified assertions. We evaluate across eight commercial LLMs and observe frequent failures on interaction-based requirements even when pages render successfully, especially on 3D simulation interfaces. Finally, we show the UI-agent harness can provide outcome-level training signals. Training compact models (e.g., Qwen2.5 14B and MIMO 7B) with RL rewards derived from executable interaction tests improves functional completion while reducing build failures.

</details>


### [169] [Self-Supervised Scaling of Terminal Environments for Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02710)
*Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Yu Wang,Jingyuan Huang,Jichao Yu,Ninghao Liu,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 software-in-the-loop reconstruction (SWR) 自监督框架，用现有软件工作流自动生成参考输出与验证目标，为终端智能体构建可扩展、行为验证的训练环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体正扩展至科学等专业领域，但为每个任务构建训练环境需可执行参考行为和领域专用验证器，反复工程成本高且难以复用。

Method: 对每个工作流执行多组输入配置，划分为公开观测与隐藏评估；智能体仅凭指令、输入模式和公开输入-输出，构建可编辑程序（无源工作流访问权限）；用分层验证器在隐藏配置上评估，结合领域语义比较、结构有效性和反捷径检查，公开反馈支持迭代修订。

Result: 实例化含 6 领域、46 个软件族、500 个工作流；每任务 3 次尝试下 Qwen3.8-Max 解决 838 个任务，产出 1,422 条验证轨迹，过采样为 3,000 个重建训练样本；微调 Qwen3.8-27B 后 Terminal-Bench 2 平均性能从 47.94% 提升至 53.56%，且在四项评测中均取得最高均值。

Conclusion: 现有科学软件可为终端智能体提供可扩展、行为验证的监督信号，无需为每个任务手工编写参考解。

Abstract: Terminal agents are increasingly deployed beyond software engineering in science and other specialized domains. Constructing training environments requires executable reference behavior and a domain-specific verifier that distinguishes semantic correctness from superficially plausible artifacts. Authoring these components for each task requires repeated engineering and limits reuse. We introduce software-in-the-loop reconstruction, a self-supervised framework that obtains reference outputs and verification targets from existing software workflows, executable programs mapping structured inputs to outputs. For each workflow, we execute multiple input configurations and partition cases into public observations and hidden evaluations. Given the instruction, input schema, and public input--output observations, an agent constructs an editable program without access to the source workflow. The candidate is evaluated on hidden configurations against workflow outputs. A hierarchical verifier combines domain-specific semantic comparison, structural validity, and anti-shortcut checks, while public feedback supports iterative revision. The construction admits additional workflows and configurations without authoring a reference solution for each task. We instantiate SWR with 500 workflows and 46 software families across six domains. Across three attempts per task, Qwen3.8-Max solves 838 tasks and produces 1,422 verified trajectories, which we oversample to 3,000 reconstruction-only training examples. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.8-27B improves mean Terminal-Bench 2 performance from 47.94% to 53.56% across three seeds and achieves the highest mean among four matched-token corpus controls on all four reported evaluations. These results indicate that existing scientific software can provide scalable, behaviorally verified supervision for terminal agents.

</details>


### [170] [VeriPy Source-Preserving Verification and Compatibility Checking for Python Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02814)
*Naing Oo Lwin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriPy 提出一套统一工作流，让带标注的 Python 组件的规约、证明与向后兼容性验证在同一流程中完成：以注释形式表达契约、不变式和证明钩子，导出 Dafny/Lean 产物，并通过关系积检查组件更新是否仍接受旧输入、保持返回值与建模异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 程序与其形式化保证在长期维护中容易脱节：规约需描述实际运行的实现，而更新又必须保留既有调用者所依赖的行为，这两项义务缺乏统一的工作流支撑。

Method: VeriPy 允许开发者与智能体以 Python 注释书写契约、不变式和证明钩子，并基于源码定位的诊断逐步精化被检查的辅助引理；直接编码器生成 Dafny 或 Lean 产物，同时保留已准入的可执行体和依赖项的显式模型；再用关系积检验更新后的组件是否仍接纳旧输入并保持返回值与建模异常。

Result: 该工作流将源码级证明开发、功能验证与向后兼容性连接起来，并记录了每条保证所依赖的假设；代码已开源于 https://github.com/astrio-labs/veripy。

Conclusion: VeriPy 为标注式 Python 组件提供了一种兼顾形式化保证与向后兼容性的统一维护工作流。

Abstract: Keeping a Python program and its formal guarantees aligned is a continuing maintenance problem. Specifications must describe the implementation that actually runs, and updates must preserve the behavior on which existing callers depend. VeriPy brings these obligations into a common workflow for annotated Python components. Developers and agents express contracts, invariants, and proof hooks as Python comments, then refine checked auxiliary lemmas using source-located diagnostics. Direct encoders produce Dafny or Lean artifacts while retaining admitted executable bodies and explicit models of their dependencies. A relational product checks whether an updated component still admits old inputs and preserves returned values and modeled exceptions. The resulting workflow connects source-level proof development with functional verification and backward compatibility, recording the assumptions under which each guarantee applies. The code is available at https://github.com/astrio-labs/veripy.

</details>


### [171] [Discriminating Fixture Coverage in Agent-Infrastructure Verification Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02928)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文量化"不变式套件仅靠单次通过/失败观察验证"的价值：在多变体测试下，标准验证认证通过的套件中有变异体存活；冻结修复后的12项检查套件对10个对抗性变异体只杀死5个。作者发现幸存者失败模式有两种（从未被激活 vs 破坏不可观测状态），并通过枚举7个输入区分维度、预先注册预测并逐维补充夹具，最终杀死全部5个幸存者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变式套件和运行时监视器越来越多地决定智能体能否部署，但对其验证通常只依赖单次观察：通过一个被认为正确的实现、失败于一个被认为错误的实现。论文旨在衡量这种单次观察到底有多大价值，并揭示标准通过/失败报告无法暴露的套件缺陷。

Method: 对多会话智能体状态投影层的不变式套件进行变异分析。先发现标准验证会认证一个让删除事件身份去重的"一阶变异体"存活的套件。修复为12项检查套件后冻结并记录哈希，用对抗性读者指定的10个变异体运行一次。再对5个幸存者进行插桩，区分其失败模式。将缺失输入视为覆盖问题，枚举输入空间的7个区分维度，预先注册哪些维度未覆盖、哪些幸存者应被解释，然后每个未覆盖维度添加一个夹具，复用原有断言，逐维杀死幸存者。

Result: 10个对抗性变异体中仅5个被杀死。5个幸存者有两种不同失败模式：3个从未被激活（无夹具提供使变异代码行为不同的输入）；2个破坏套件中所有断言都无法观测的内部状态。按7个区分维度补充夹具后，全部5个幸存者均被杀死，且每个都由其预测维度对应的检查杀死。

Conclusion: 单次通过/失败观察的验证价值有限，会遗漏去重类变异体；幸存者的"未被激活"与"不可观测状态破坏"两种模式需要不同修复，且都不可见于通过/失败报告。通过将缺失输入作为覆盖问题、预先注册预测并逐维补充夹具可有效修复。作者将其定位为同一挑战集上的修复结果而非第二次留出估计，并提供含冻结哈希、注册预测、变异体和运行日志的工件以保证可核查性。

Abstract: Invariant suites and runtime monitors increasingly gate agent deployment decisions, and the evidence offered for any particular suite is almost always a single observation: it passes an implementation believed correct and fails one believed broken. We measure what that observation is worth. Applying mutation analysis to an invariant suite for a multi-session agent state-projection layer, we first find that this standard validation certifies a suite in which a first-order mutant removing event-identity deduplication survives every check. We then freeze the repaired twelve-check suite, record its hash, and run it once against ten mutants specified by an adversarial reader who designed none of its fixtures: it kills five. Instrumenting the five survivors against the reference shows they fail in two distinct ways, not one. Three are never activated, because no fixture supplies an input on which the mutated code behaves differently at all. The other two corrupt internal state that no oracle in the suite can observe. The two modes need different repairs, and neither is visible from a pass/fail report. Treating the missing inputs as a coverage question, we enumerate seven discriminating dimensions of the input space, register in advance which are uncovered and which survivors they should explain, and add one fixture per uncovered dimension while reusing the existing oracles verbatim. All five survivors then die, each to the check written for its predicted dimension. We report this as a repair result on the same challenge set rather than a second held-out estimate, and give the artifact, including the frozen hash, the registered predictions, all mutants and the run logs, so the distinction is checkable.

</details>


### [172] [GTDD: Generative Test-Driven Development for AI Coding Agents with Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02952)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式测试驱动开发（GTDD）：测试智能体在每轮实现固定后生成新输入，可信评估器验证并回传精简反例、存入回归测试，使开发持续面对初始样例之外的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体可针对已观察示例调整实现，因此固定测试集可能通过但仍有大量预期行为未实现，需要防止这种"虚假通过"。

Method: 将TDD重构为生成式流程：独立测试智能体依据人类指定的行为契约与历史反馈生成新输入；可信评估器校验输入、向编码智能体返回精简反例并保存为回归测试；用有限总体分析刻画自适应候选选择下的错误接受界限。

Result: 理论界限量化了测试可见性与重复反馈对接受率的影响，表明候选实现固定后的新鲜随机审计可跨轮控制错误接受；在有状态键值存储的配对实验中，开发中重新生成测试的策略平均失败率低于同模型一次性生成测试的策略，而向测试者提供候选源码无显著额外提升；等量测试条件未能隔离出差异来源。

Conclusion: GTDD将自适应开发反馈与既有回归测试、独立接受规则结合，可有效抑制自适应实现带来的虚假接受。

Abstract: Test-driven development gives AI coding agents executable requirements for implementing software. Because these agents can adapt their implementations to the examples they observe, passing a predetermined collection of tests can leave substantial parts of the intended behavior unimplemented. We propose Generative Test-Driven Development (GTDD), a formulation of test-driven development in which a separate testing agent generates new inputs after each candidate implementation is fixed, using a human-specified behavioral contract and the feedback from earlier rounds. A trusted evaluator checks these inputs, returns reduced counterexamples to the coding agent, and saves them for regression testing, so development continually confronts failures beyond the initial examples. We characterize the evidence that this process provides through a finite-population analysis of false acceptance under adaptive candidate selection. The resulting bounds quantify how test visibility and repeated feedback affect acceptance, and show that fresh random audits after candidate commitment control false acceptance across development rounds. In a paired experiment on a stateful key-value store, both policies that regenerated tests during development ended with lower mean failure rates than the policy whose tests were generated once by the same language model, and giving the tester the candidate's source produced no detectable additional improvement. Further conditions requesting equal numbers of tests did not isolate any single feature of the policies as the source of this difference. GTDD combines this adaptive development feedback with established regression tests and an independent acceptance rule.

</details>


### [173] [BISCEPTER: Probability-Driven Bisection for Large-Scale System Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02995)
*Mingyan Gao,Celine Wüst,Zuming Jiang,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 BISCEPTER，一种概率驱动的二分查找方法，利用历史 BIC 延迟作为先验，通过选择加权中位数枢轴来分割 BIC 概率质量而非提交数量，从而显著减少定位引入缺陷提交所需的迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二分查找选择剩余区间的中位数提交以平衡提交数量，其假设每个提交是 BIC 的概率相等。然而该假设与真实世界的 BIC 历史不符，实际存在强烈的时间偏斜，即 BIC 更可能出现在报告时间附近的近期提交中。

Method: 构建了包含 GCC、Linux 内核和 MariaDB 的 8,172 个缺陷报告数据集，发现 50% 的 BIC 位于报告时间前最近 0.69% 的提交历史中。基于此观察，提出 BISCEPTER，使用历史 BIC 延迟作为轻量级先验，选择加权中位数枢轴来分割估计的 BIC 概率质量，同时保持与标准二分查找相同的 good-bad 预言机和接口。

Result: 在三个大型系统上评估，BISCEPTER 相比标准中位数二分查找平均减少 25.75% 的二分迭代次数（最高达 55.55%），并在 91.26% 的测试用例中优于基线。鲁棒性实验表明，在噪声历史数据下其优势依然稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究能有效节省实际软件调试的工作量，并通过揭示 BIC 分布的相关见解，更广泛地惠及未来软件工程研究。

Abstract: Identifying the bug-inducing commit (BIC) is a fundamental step in regression debugging and a key input to emerging BIC-aware fault-localization pipelines. In practice, BICs are commonly obtained with bisection. Standard bisection selects the median commit of the remaining good-bad interval, thereby balancing commit count. This strategy is optimal under the assumption that each commit is equally likely to be the BIC.
  This paper shows that this assumption does not match real-world BIC histories. We construct a dataset of 8,172 bug reports from GCC, the Linux kernel, and MariaDB. We find a strong temporal skew: across the studied systems, 50% of BICs lie within the most recent 0.69% of the report-time commit history. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BISCEPTER, a probability-driven bisection approach that uses historical BIC latency as a lightweight prior. Instead of selecting pivots that split the number of remaining commits, BISCEPTER selects weighted-median pivots that split estimated BIC probability mass, while preserving the same good-bad oracle and interface as standard bisection. We evaluate BISCEPTER on three large-scale systems. The evaluation results show that BISCEPTER reduces bisection iterations by 25.75% on average (up to 55.55%) compared with standard median bisection, while improving over the baseline in 91.26% of test cases. Robustness experiments further demonstrate that the benefit remains stable under noisy historical data. We expect that our research can effectively save effort in debugging software in practice and, more broadly, benefit future software engineering research by bringing insights about BIC distribution.

</details>


### [174] [Engineering Sustainable Agents: A Systematic Comparison of Agentic LLMs for Developer Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03010)
*Merve Astekin,Yan Naing Tun,Arda Goknil,Erik Johannes Husom,Lwin Khin Shar,Hasan Sözer,Ratnadira Widyasari,Hui Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对五类软件工程任务，系统比较非智能体到多智能体LLM配置的准确性、延迟与能耗，发现多智能体能耗/耗时显著更高而精度收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程中日益普及，智能体系统协调多智能体但计算与环境成本更高，缺乏对智能体复杂度与能耗效率权衡的系统性实证研究。

Method: 针对代码生成、技术债识别、代码漏洞检测、日志解析、日志分析五任务，采用6个开源权重LLM、2种提示策略、3种硬件平台，比较从单查询非智能体基线到多智能体工作流的配置，评估准确性、推理延迟与能耗。

Result: 多智能体平均消耗6.36倍能耗、运行时间6.07倍，最差任务-硬件组合慢至160倍；精度提升有限且任务相关，仅漏洞检测有提升，Pareto前沿66个最优配置中59个为非/单智能体；模型与提示选择效果因任务而异。

Conclusion: 智能体复杂度与能效存在显著权衡，需面向任务与可持续性设计LLM开发工具，而非默认使用多智能体。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering, including agentic systems that coordinate multiple agents, but impose higher computational and environmental costs. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study of agentic LLM systems across five software engineering tasks: code generation, technical debt identification, code vulnerability detection, log parsing, and log analysis. For each task, we compare LLM configurations that range from a non-agentic single-query baseline to multi-agent workflows, using six open-weight LLMs, two prompt strategies, and three hardware platforms. We assess each configuration in terms of accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption. Our results reveal substantial trade-offs between agentic complexity and energy efficiency: multi-agent designs consume on average 6.36$\times$ as much energy and run 6.07$\times$ as long as the non-agentic baseline, with worst-case slowdowns of up to 160$\times$ for individual task--hardware pairs. Accuracy gains from additional agents are limited and task-specific: multi-agent improves average vulnerability-detection accuracy, but lightweight non-agentic and single-agent configurations still dominate the Pareto front, accounting for 59 of 66 Pareto-optimal configurations. Model and prompt choice act as task-specific levers whose effective direction varies between tasks rather than as global defaults. We translate these findings into design guidelines for sustainable, task-aware LLM-based development tools.

</details>


### [175] [MintEval: Do LLMs Implement the Trading Strategy You Asked For? A Behavioural-Equivalence Benchmark for Natural-Language-to-Strategy Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03080)
*Siyu Wang,Yifan Wang,Yuecheng He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MintEval基准，从行为而非代码相似度评估LLM从口语化交易指令实现策略代码的能力，揭示普遍存在的"静默失败"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从生成交易信号转向编写执行代码，其失败模式是静默的：生成的代码能运行、回测能画图，但执行的风险逻辑与交易者描述的并不一致。现有代码基准只测单元测试的功能正确性，金融基准只测预测，均无法衡量实现是否"按所要求的策略"行事。

Method: MintEval从可组合的构建块库以程序化方式生成参考策略，反向翻译成口语化交易者指令，再由被测模型重新实现。生成程序与参考程序在相同行情数据与摩擦条件下逐bar执行，按"动作"而非代码相似度或利润进行比较（alpha被差分消除）。v0含800个BTCUSDT 15分钟任务，按执行度量的状态跨度复杂度τ分层，且τ与描述长度解耦。

Result: 低成本模型平均ActionMatch最高仅0.544，最多0.087任务完全复现；200任务分层子集上前沿模型(Claude Opus 5.5)达0.889、0.575完全复现，但仍有0.275任务静默失败。给定构建块菜单时模型几乎能完美识别策略，但79.2%规格被正确理解的实现，在超过10%的活跃bar上发生偏离。某近期策略生成基准的LLM评判器照搬使用，却接受了所有这些静默失败。

Conclusion: 现有评估无法捕捉"策略实现语义偏差"这一静默失败，MintEval以行为对齐度量填补这一空白。研究警示：即便模型能正确读出策略意图，代码实现仍常偏离，且当前LLM评判器会漏检这些错误。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from producing trading signals to writing the code that executes them. The failure mode of the second role is silent: generated code runs, a backtest plots, yet the risk logic that the trader described is not the logic being executed. Existing code benchmarks test functional correctness on unit tests and finance benchmarks test forecasting; neither measures whether an implementation behaves like the strategy that was asked for. We introduce MintEval, a benchmark in which reference strategies are generated programmatically from a library of composable building blocks, back-translated into colloquial trader instructions, and re-implemented by the model under test. Generated and reference programs are executed bar by bar on identical market data and frictions, and compared on their actions rather than on code similarity or profit: alpha is differenced away. MintEval v0 contains 800 tasks on BTCUSDT 15-minute data, stratified by an execution-measured state-span complexity tau that is decoupled from description length. Low-cost models reach a mean ActionMatch of at most 0.544 and reproduce at most 0.087 of tasks exactly; on a stratified subset of 200 tasks a frontier model (Claude Opus 5.5) reaches 0.889 and reproduces 0.575 exactly, yet still fails silently on 0.275 of tasks. Given a menu of building blocks, models identify the strategy almost perfectly, yet 79.2% of the implementations whose specification was read correctly diverge on more than 10% of active bars. The LLM judge of a recent strategy-generation benchmark, applied verbatim, accepts every one of these silent failures.

</details>


### [176] [Pinning Decisions Before Failure: Executable Records of Underspecified Choices in AI-Assisted Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03237)
*Takeharu Mitsui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"决策钉"（decision pin）方法，通过枚举需求中的欠定点、构造仅在单点上分歧的两个一次性实现来找到区分性输入，并将其记录为约束生成与执行合规的凭据，在40项基准上取得92.5%的区分输入覆盖率与85-90%的意图识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求存在欠定性，模型实现时会静默地做出选择；620个测试套件中42.7%不含能区分不同解读的测试。事先写验收示例是常见补救手段，但两种解读都满足的示例无法揭示分歧，因此需要一种能显式暴露并锁定欠定决策的机制。

Method: 在实践流程中插入两步：首先按名称枚举需求的欠定点，然后针对每个欠定点构造两个仅在该点上不同的临时实现，仅当执行二者能显示分歧时才保留该候选输入；所得记录称为"决策钉"，包含命名点、确认输入与用户在两个展示结果间选择的值，该记录同时用于约束生成并通过执行判定合规。

Result: 在40项带成对参考实现和两个模型的基准上，92.5%的决策点获得区分性输入，85-90%的钉能识别出意图决策。对703个独立生成实现的检验中，钉与基准分类一致率为96-97%，仅3个任务出现分歧（其中一个两者皆错）。作为生成约束，210次生成全部在钉定输入上被遵守，38个单元中在留出输入上不劣于散文规则，但也不优于陈述相同范围的散文。

Conclusion: 散文规则下的合规失败均源于模型自行决定规则范围；其中一例静默推翻了另一条已记录决策，经两模型留一法归因于单一规则，文本级调和未能发现而重放记录输入可捕获。当前设定下此类冲突过少，无法评估回归步骤，作者对此作出了说明。

Abstract: A natural-language requirement leaves questions open, and a model asked to implement it settles them silently: across 600 generated test suites from three models, 42.7% contain no test that distinguishes the competing readings. An acceptance example written before implementing is the usual remedy, but an example both readings satisfy resolves nothing.
  We insert two steps into that practice: enumerate the requirement's underspecified points by name, then for each construct two throwaway implementations differing only in that point and keep a candidate input only if executing both shows they disagree. The result is recorded as a decision pin: the named point, the confirmed input, and the value the person chose between the two exhibited results. The same record then constrains generation and decides compliance by execution.
  On a benchmark of 40 tasks with paired reference implementations and two models, a separating input is obtained for 92.5% of decision points and a pin identifying the intended decision for 85-90%. As checks on 703 independently generated implementations, pins agree with the benchmark's classification on 96-97%, with disagreements on three tasks, one where both classifiers erred. As generation constraints, pins are honoured at the pinned input in all 210 generations and are never worse than a prose rule on held-out inputs in 38 cells, though no better than prose stating the same scope.
  Every compliance failure under a prose rule came from the model deciding the rule's scope itself; one such case silently overturned another recorded decision, was attributed to a single rule by leave-one-out on both models, and was missed by text-level reconciliation but caught by re-running the recorded input. The setting yields too few such conflicts to evaluate a regression step, and we say why.

</details>


### [177] [IntentCoding: Amplifying User Intent in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00066)
*Zheng Fang,Yihong Dong,Lili Mou,Dongming Jin,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出IntentCoding解码策略，通过掩码捕捉并放大用户意图影响，提升LLM在多约束代码生成中遵循意图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成能力强，但在多约束下遵循细粒度用户意图仍困难。经验分析发现：约束越多性能急剧下降；用户意图虽影响logits，但不足以有效引导解码。

Method: IntentCoding：掩码用户意图以捕捉其影响，采用多强度集成机制放大意图作用。模型无关、无需训练、可无缝集成现有解码流程。另构建CodeConstraints基准测试多约束意图遵循。

Result: 在CodeConstraints上相对提升最高71.0%，IFEvalCode最高67.3%，HumanEval和LiveCodeBench上pass@1相对贪心解码提升最高29.3%，约束满足与功能正确性均显著改善。

Conclusion: IntentCoding有效提升LLM对用户意图的遵循，兼具通用性与实用性，为多约束代码生成提供新解码思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code generation, but their adherence to fine-grained user intent with multiple constraints remains a significant challenge. Our empirical analysis reveals two key observations: 1) Model performance deteriorates quickly as the number of constraints in the user intent increases, and 2) While user intent does influence the model's logits, such an influence may not be strong enough to effectively steer the decoding process. To this end, we propose Intent-Amplified Code Generation (IntentCoding), a novel decoding strategy that enhances an LLM's ability to follow user intent. IntentCoding captures the influence of user intent by masking out the intent, and applies a multi-strength ensemble mechanism to amplify the effect of user intent during generation. IntentCoding is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and integrates seamlessly with existing decoding procedures. To enable systematic evaluation, we also construct CodeConstraints, a benchmark dataset specifically designed to test user intent compliance under varying numbers of constraints. Experiments on our constructed Constraints, as well as popular IFEvalCode, HumanEval and LiveCodeBench datasets, show that our IntentCoding model significantly improves both constraint satisfaction and functional correctness compared to standard decoding approaches. IntentCoding achieves up to 71.0% relative improvement on CodeConstraints, achieves up to 67.3% relative improvement on IFEvalCode and achieves up to 29.3% relative improvement in pass@1 on HumanEval and LiveCodeBench compared with greedy decoding.

</details>


### [178] [ClarifyCodeBench: Evaluating LLMs on Clarifying Ambiguous Requirements for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00711)
*Zheng Fang,Dongming Jin,Yihong dong,Yongmin Li,Kechi Zhang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ClarifyCodeBench 交互式基准，评估LLM澄清需求歧义的能力，揭示代码生成与需求澄清能力解耦、推理提升有限、多歧义场景性能骤降三大发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为编程助手时，代码生成效果受限于输入需求质量，而需求常模糊、不完整或欠明确。现有基准假设prompt完美，忽视了需求 elicit 的交互式瓶颈，LLM主动澄清意图的能力未被充分探索。

Method: 构建 ClarifyCodeBench：基于真实编程任务，含人工标注的独特歧义类型、澄清问题及标准答案；形式化两个交互质量指标——Turn-discounted Key Question Rate（惩罚低效提问）和 Optimal Round Adherence（衡量 elicit 精度）；对六个SOTA LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 三大发现：1) 能力解耦——强代码生成不代表有效需求澄清；2) 推理悖论——增加思考提升代码正确性，但对歧义识别增益有限；3) 多歧义天花板——歧义密度增加时澄清性能急剧下降，暴露复杂规格处理的瓶颈。

Conclusion: AI4SE研究需从静态代码合成转向交互式需求 elicit。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as programming assistants. However, the efficacy of code generation is constrained by the quality of input requirements, which are frequently ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. While LLMs excel at one-shot code synthesis, their ability to proactively clarify intent remains underexplored, as a critical trait for robust software engineering. Existing benchmarks largely overlook this interactive bottleneck, assuming perfectly specified prompts that do not reflect the iterative nature of requirement elicitation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ClarifyCodeBench, a novel interactive benchmark for evaluating LLMs' capability in resolving requirement ambiguity. Constructed from real-world programming tasks, ClarifyCodeBench features high-quality manual annotations, including N unique ambiguity types, associated clarification questions, and corresponding ground-truth answers. Furthermore, we formalize two rigorous metrics to assess the interaction quality: Turn-discounted Key Question Rate, which penalizes inefficient questioning, and Optimal Round Adherence, which measures the precision of the elicitation process. We conduct a systematic evaluation of six state-of-the-art LLMs using ClarifyCodeBench. Our empirical results yield three critical insights: 1) Capability Decoupling: Strong code generation performance does not inherently translate to effective requirement clarification; 2) The Reasoning Paradox: While increased computational thinking enhances code correctness, it yields marginal gains in identifying ambiguities; 3) The Multi-ambiguity Ceiling: LLMs' clarification performance degrades sharply as the density of ambiguities increases, revealing a significant bottleneck in handling complex, real-world specifications. Our work underscores the necessity for future AI4SE research to transition from static synthesis to interactive elicitation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Coherence-Driven Belief Formation and Population Dynamics of Contagion in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02654)
*Tathagata Banerjee,Nima Moghaddas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证测量了语言模型智能体的信念采纳概率与同伴支持数之间的关系，发现采纳函数呈S形（复杂传染特征），阈值受可信度、来源可靠性与智能体倾向影响，并可归结为单一"一致性"维度；集体动力学中出现了复杂传染的典型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有社会传染模型多假设个体如何采纳信念并从个体行为推导群体行为，缺乏对信念采纳机制的实证测量。本文转而直接测量语言模型智能体中的信念采纳过程。

Method: 以语言模型智能体为对象，经验性测量其在给定同伴支持数量下采纳某主张的概率，得到"采纳核"；分析阈值对主张合理性、来源可靠性与智能体倾向三个来源的敏感性，并检验三者能否归约为单一有效维度；在AI智能体系统中比较集群网络与随机网络上的传播，并考察级联窗口与滞后共识现象。

Result: 采纳核呈S形，属复杂传染特征；阈值受主张合理性、来源可靠性与智能体倾向影响，这三者可被单一有效维度近似，理解为传入信念与LLM智能体先验信念的一致性。集体动力学显示：集群网络传播强于随机网络；存在分叉的级联窗口；出现自我维持的滞后共识，导致共识的移除远比建立困难。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体中的信念采纳与传播呈现出复杂传染的典型规律，且可用"一致性"这一有效维度加以刻画，为理解AI智能体群体中的共识形成与顽固性提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Models of social contagion usually assume how individuals adopt beliefs and derive population behavior from it. We instead empirically measure belief adoption in language model agents, quantifying the probability an agent adopts a claim given how many peers endorse it. We find this adoption kernel to be sigmoid, a characteristic of complex contagion, with a threshold that is sensitive to three sources: the claim's plausibility, the source's reliability, and the agent's disposition. These three dimensions are well approximated by a single effective dimension which we propose can be understood as the coherence of the incoming belief with the LLM agent's prior beliefs.
